    index0 = 768*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):

        load = ops.load('addmm_36', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('addmm_36', get_index_1)
        mul = ops.mul(load, load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(-0.07135481627260025, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(-1.5957691216057308, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, constant_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('addmm_36', get_index_2)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(add, load_2)
        exp = ops.exp(mul_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('addmm_36', get_index_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_4 = ops.load('addmm_36', get_index_4)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load_3, load_4)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(-0.07135481627260025, torch.float32)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(mul_3, constant_2)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(-1.5957691216057308, torch.float32)

        exp_1 = ops.exp(mul_5)

        add_2 = ops.add(exp_1, constant_4)
        reciprocal = ops.reciprocal(add_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)

class op234_loop_body:

    index0 = 16384*p0 + 128*p1 + p2
    index1 = 128*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        eq = ops.eq(constant, constant_1)
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf232', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('div_4', get_index_1)
        mul = ops.mul(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('div_4', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf233', get_index_3)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load_2, load_3)
        sub = ops.sub(mul, mul_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(eq, constant_2, sub)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf234', get_index_4, where, None)
        return store

op261: SchedulerNode(ComputedBuffer)

class op261_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 128, p1: 3072}
    index0 = 3072*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_1)
        mul = ops.mul(load, load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(-0.07135481627260025, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(-1.5957691216057308, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, constant_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_2)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(add, load_2)
        exp = ops.exp(mul_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_4 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_4)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load_3, load_4)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(-0.07135481627260025, torch.float32)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(mul_3, constant_2)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(-1.5957691216057308, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(mul_4, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_5 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_5)
        mul_5 = ops.mul(add_1, load_5)
        exp_1 = ops.exp(mul_5)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_2 = ops.add(exp_1, constant_4)
        reciprocal = ops.reciprocal(add_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_6 = ops.mul(reciprocal, constant_5)
        mul_7 = ops.mul(exp, mul_6)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_6 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_6)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_7 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_7)
        mul_8 = ops.mul(load_6, load_7)
        constant_6 = ops.constant(0.2140644488178007, torch.float32)
        mul_9 = ops.mul(mul_8, constant_6)
        constant_7 = ops.constant(1.5957691216057308, torch.float32)
        add_3 = ops.add(mul_9, constant_7)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_8 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_8)
        mul_10 = ops.mul(add_3, load_8)
        mul_11 = ops.mul(mul_7, mul_10)
        get_index_9 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_9 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_9)
        get_index_10 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_10 = ops.load('addmm_4', get_index_10)

        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf279', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('div_1', get_index_1)

        where = ops.where(eq, constant_2, sub)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf281', get_index_4, where, None)
        return store
